[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +  /**
[tv-dator]         +   * Distributed transaction coordinator with participant state persistence hooks.
[tv-dator]         +   */
[tv-dator]         +  class DistributedTransactionCoordinator {
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} options - Coordinator options.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} options.beginParticipant - Begin callback.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.prepareParticipant] - Prepare callback.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} options.commitParticipant - Commit callback.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} options.rollbackParticipant - Rollback callback.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.persistTransaction] - Persist tx row callback.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.persistParticipant] - Persist participant callback.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.persistWriteOperation] - Persist write op callback.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.persistTransactionDecision] - Record the
[tv-dator]         +     *   transaction's insert-once decision; throws when one is already
[tv-dator]         +     *   recorded or the write's outcome is unknown.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.readTransactionDecision] - Read the recorded
[tv-dator]         +     *   decision row of a transaction (null when none is readable).
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.epochSource] - Monotonic transaction epoch source.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {number} [options.transactionBudgetMs] - Transaction timeout budget.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {number} [options.participantRetryMaxRetries] - Retry attempts.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {number} [options.participantRetryBaseDelayMs] - Retry base delay.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {number} [options.participantRetryMaxDelayMs] - Retry max delay.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.sleep] - Async sleep hook.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.onParticipantRetry] - Retry diagnostic hook.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Function} [options.now] - Clock function.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    constructor(options = {}) {
[tv-dator]         +      this.beginParticipant = options.beginParticipant || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.prepareParticipant = options.prepareParticipant || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.commitParticipant = options.commitParticipant || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.rollbackParticipant = options.rollbackParticipant || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.persistTransaction = options.persistTransaction || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.persistParticipant = options.persistParticipant || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.persistWriteOperation =
[tv-dator]         +        options.persistWriteOperation || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.persistTransactionParticipantSet = options.persistTransactionParticipantSet ||
[tv-dator]         +        (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.readTransactionRecoveryState = options.readTransactionRecoveryState || null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.persistTransactionDecision =
[tv-dator]         +        options.persistTransactionDecision || (async () => {});
[tv-dator]         +      this.readTransactionDecision =
[tv-dator]         +        typeof options.readTransactionDecision === 'function' ?
[tv-dator]         +          options.readTransactionDecision :
[tv-dator]         +          null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.resolveParticipantCommitOutcome =
[tv-dator]         +        typeof options.resolveParticipantCommitOutcome === 'function' ?
[tv-dator]         +          options.resolveParticipantCommitOutcome :
[tv-dator]         +          null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.now = options.now || (() => Date.now());
[tv-dator]         +      this.nextEpoch = Number.isFinite(options.initialEpoch) ?
[tv-dator]         +        Math.floor(options.initialEpoch) :
[tv-dator]         +        this.now();
[tv-dator]         +      this.epochSource =
[tv-dator]         +        options.epochSource ||
[tv-dator]         +        (() => {
[tv-dator]         +          this.nextEpoch += 1;
[tv-dator]         +          return this.nextEpoch;
[tv-dator]         +        });
[tv-dator]         +      this.transactionBudgetMs =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.transactionBudgetMs) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.transactionBudgetMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.transactionBudgetMs) :
[tv-dator]         +          TIMEOUT_BUDGET_DEFAULT.TRANSACTION_BUDGET_MS;
[tv-dator]         +      this.participantRetryMaxRetries =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.participantRetryMaxRetries) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.participantRetryMaxRetries >= 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.participantRetryMaxRetries) :
[tv-dator]         +          PARTICIPANT_RETRY_DEFAULT.MAX_RETRIES;
[tv-dator]         +      this.participantRetryBaseDelayMs =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.participantRetryBaseDelayMs) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.participantRetryBaseDelayMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.participantRetryBaseDelayMs) :
[tv-dator]         +          PARTICIPANT_RETRY_DEFAULT.BASE_DELAY_MS;
[tv-dator]         +      this.participantRetryMaxDelayMs =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.participantRetryMaxDelayMs) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.participantRetryMaxDelayMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.participantRetryMaxDelayMs) :
[tv-dator]         +          PARTICIPANT_RETRY_DEFAULT.MAX_DELAY_MS;
[tv-dator]         +      this.sleep =
[tv-dator]         +        options.sleep ||
[tv-dator]         +        ((delayMs) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs)));
[tv-dator]         +      this.onParticipantRetry = options.onParticipantRetry || null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.logger = options.logger || console;
[tv-dator]         +      this.loadRecoveryStateForSweep = options.loadRecoveryStateForSweep || null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback =
[tv-dator]         +        typeof options.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback === 'function' ?
[tv-dator]         +          options.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback :
[tv-dator]         +          null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepIntervalMs =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.recoverySweepIntervalMs) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.recoverySweepIntervalMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.recoverySweepIntervalMs) :
[tv-dator]         +          RECOVERY_SWEEP_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepTimer = null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepInFlight = false;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs = 0;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepDeferredAttempts = 0;
[tv-dator]         +      this.workflowCoordinator =
[tv-dator]         +        options.workflowCoordinator ||
[tv-dator]         +        new DurableWorkflowCoordinator({
[tv-dator]         +          // The transaction row is inserted once, before any participant
[tv-dator]         +          // work; every later write of it updates the existing row.
[tv-dator]         +          persistWorkflow: async (workflow) => {
[tv-dator]         +            await this.persistTransaction({
[tv-dator]         +              transactionId: workflow.transactionId || workflow.workflowId,
[tv-dator]         +              sessionId: workflow.sessionId || workflow.ownerKey,
[tv-dator]         +              status: workflow.status,
[tv-dator]         +              transactionEpoch: workflow.transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +              timeoutDeadline: workflow.timeoutDeadline,
[tv-dator]         +              transactionMode: workflow.transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +              participantSetState: workflow.participantSetState,
[tv-dator]         +              participantSetText: workflow.participantSetText,
[tv-dator]         +              commitMode: workflow.commitMode,
[tv-dator]         +              frozenParticipantCount: workflow.frozenParticipantCount,
[tv-dator]         +              createdAt: workflow.createdAt,
[tv-dator]         +              updatedAt: workflow.updatedAt,
[tv-dator]         +            }, {insertOnce: workflow.transactionRowInserted !== true});
[tv-dator]         +            workflow.transactionRowInserted = true;
[tv-dator]         +          },
[tv-dator]         +          persistParticipant: async (participant) => {
[tv-dator]         +            await this.persistParticipant({
[tv-dator]         +              participantId: participant.participantId,
[tv-dator]         +              transactionId: participant.transactionId || participant.workflowId,
[tv-dator]         +              partitionId: participant.partitionId || participant.participantKey,
[tv-dator]         +              status: participant.status,
[tv-dator]         +              lastError: participant.lastError,
[tv-dator]         +              preparedDigest: participant.preparedDigest ?? null,
[tv-dator]         +              prepareIndex: participant.prepareIndex ?? null,
[tv-dator]         +              prepareTerm: participant.prepareTerm ?? null,
[tv-dator]         +              createdAt: participant.createdAt,
[tv-dator]         +              updatedAt: participant.updatedAt,
[tv-dator]         +            });
[tv-dator]         +            if (participant.status === PARTICIPANT_STATUS.PREPARED) {
[tv-dator]         +              participant.prepareEvidenceRetained = true;
[tv-dator]         +            }
[tv-dator]         +          },
[tv-dator]         +          now: this.now,
[tv-dator]         +        });
[tv-dator]         +      this.transactionsBySession = this.workflowCoordinator.workflowsByOwnerKey;
[tv-dator]         +      // Each session's last ended transaction: a COMMIT that finds none reads
[tv-dator]         +      // whether that transaction reached its commit point from here.
[tv-dator]         +      this.endedTransactions = createEndedTransactionRecords();
[tv-dator]         +      this.transactionOperationTailBySession = new Map();
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoveredTransactionIds = new Set();
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Begin a distributed transaction for a session.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} [options] - {transactionMode, timeoutDeadline}: a
[tv-dator]         +     *   retried statement-autocommit attempt names its first attempt's
[tv-dator]         +     *   deadline and keeps it (TX1 seam V: one end-to-end budget).
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<Object>} Transaction begin result.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async begin(sessionId, options = {}) {
[tv-dator]         +      const expectedTransactionId = options.expectedTransactionId ??
[tv-dator]         +        this.getTransaction(sessionId)?.transactionId;
[tv-dator]         +      return this.runTransactionOperation(sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        () => this.beginOwned(sessionId, {...options, expectedTransactionId}));
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    async beginOwned(sessionId, options) {
[tv-dator]         +      const admission = admitExpectedTransaction(this, sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        options.expectedTransactionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (admission.state === STATEMENT_ADMISSION.REFUSED) return admission.failure;
[tv-dator]         +      if (this.transactionsBySession.has(sessionId)) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.TRANSACTION_ACTIVE,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.TRANSACTION_ACTIVE,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      let transactionEpoch;
[tv-dator]         +      try {
[tv-dator]         +        transactionEpoch = this.epochSource();
[tv-dator]         +      } catch (_err) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.BEGIN_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.INTERNAL_ERROR,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +      if (!isCanonicalTransactionEpoch(transactionEpoch)) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.BEGIN_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.INTERNAL_ERROR,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      const timeoutBudget = createTopLevelOperationBudget({
[tv-dator]         +        configuredBudgetMs: this.transactionBudgetMs,
[tv-dator]         +        operationName: QUERY_OPERATION.TRANSACTION,
[tv-dator]         +        now: this.now,
[tv-dator]         +        startedAtMs: Number.isFinite(options.timeoutDeadline) ?
[tv-dator]         +          options.timeoutDeadline - this.transactionBudgetMs : this.now(),
[tv-dator]         +      });
[tv-dator]         +      const transactionMode =
[tv-dator]         +        options.transactionMode === TRANSACTION_MODE.AUTOCOMMIT ?
[tv-dator]         +          TRANSACTION_MODE.AUTOCOMMIT :
[tv-dator]         +          TRANSACTION_MODE.EXPLICIT;
[tv-dator]         +      return this.registerUniqueTransaction(sessionId, {
[tv-dator]         +        transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget, transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +      });
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    async registerUniqueTransaction(sessionId, fields) {
[tv-dator]         +      const {transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget, transactionMode} = fields;
[tv-dator]         +      for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt += 1) {
[tv-dator]         +        const transactionId = this.createTransactionId(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +        try {
[tv-dator]         +          await this.registerTransaction(sessionId, transactionId, {
[tv-dator]         +            transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +            timeoutBudget,
[tv-dator]         +            transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +          });
[tv-dator]         +        } catch (error) {
[tv-dator]         +          if (error?.code === QUERY_ERROR_CODE.TRANSACTION_RECOVERY_INCOMPLETE &&
[tv-dator]         +              error.advancedTransactionRow &&
[tv-dator]         +              this.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback !== null) {
[tv-dator]         +            try {
[tv-dator]         +              this.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback(
[tv-dator]         +                error.advancedTransactionRow,
[tv-dator]         +              );
[tv-dator]         +            } catch (recoveryError) {
[tv-dator]         +              this.logger?.warn?.(QUERY_ERROR_MSG.TRANSACTION_RECOVERY_INCOMPLETE,
[tv-dator]         +                {transactionId, error: recoveryError?.message});
[tv-dator]         +            }
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]         +          // Only a confirmed collision of the minted identity re-mints; any
[tv-dator]         +          // other persistence failure is a typed BEGIN failure (seam B/S).
[tv-dator]         +          if (isTransactionIdCollision(error) &&
[tv-dator]         +              attempt < TRANSACTION_ID_MINT_ATTEMPTS) {
[tv-dator]         +            continue;
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]         +          return {
[tv-dator]         +            success: false,
[tv-dator]         +            error: error?.message || QUERY_ERROR_MSG.BEGIN_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +            errorCode: error?.code || error?.errorCode ||
[tv-dator]         +              QUERY_ERROR_CODE.TRANSACTION_STATE_PERSIST_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +          };
[tv-dator]         +        }
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: true,
[tv-dator]         +          operation: QUERY_OPERATION.BEGIN_TRANSACTION,
[tv-dator]         +          sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +          transactionId,
[tv-dator]         +          transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +          transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Insert the transaction's row once, before any participant work, and
[tv-dator]         +     * hold the transaction for its session.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} transactionId - The minted identity.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} fields - {transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget,
[tv-dator]         +     *   transactionMode}.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<void>}
[tv-dator]         +     * @private
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async registerTransaction(sessionId, transactionId, fields) {
[tv-dator]         +      const {transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget, transactionMode} = fields;
[tv-dator]         +      const now = this.now();
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = {
[tv-dator]         +        sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        ownerKey: sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionId,
[tv-dator]         +        workflowId: transactionId,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +        timeoutBudget,
[tv-dator]         +        timeoutDeadline: timeoutBudget.deadlineMs,
[tv-dator]         +        status: TRANSACTION_STATUS.ACTIVE,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetState: PARTICIPANT_SET_STATE.OPEN,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetText: JSON.stringify([]),
[tv-dator]         +        commitMode: COMMIT_MODE.NOT_SELECTED,
[tv-dator]         +        frozenParticipantCount: 0,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetFrozenAt: 0,
[tv-dator]         +        decisionTrace: [],
[tv-dator]         +        participants: new Map(),
[tv-dator]         +        writeOperations: [],
[tv-dator]         +        createdAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +        updatedAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]         +      await this.workflowCoordinator.registerWorkflow(tx);
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Enlist partition participants and begin transaction on new participants.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string[]} partitionIds - Target partition IDs.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<Object>} Enlistment result.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async enlistParticipants(sessionId, partitionIds,
[tv-dator]         +      expectedTransactionId = this.getTransaction(sessionId)?.transactionId) {
[tv-dator]         +      return this.runTransactionOperation(
[tv-dator]         +        sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        () => {
[tv-dator]         +          const admission = admitExpectedTransaction(this, sessionId, expectedTransactionId);
[tv-dator]         +          return admission.state === STATEMENT_ADMISSION.REFUSED ? admission.failure :
[tv-dator]         +            this.enlistParticipantsOwned(sessionId, partitionIds);
[tv-dator]         +        },
[tv-dator]         +      );
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Record distributed write operation metadata under a transaction.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} operation - Operation metadata.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<void>}
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async recordWriteOperation(sessionId, operation) {
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = this.transactionsBySession.get(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (!tx) {
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      const now = this.now();
[tv-dator]         +      const normalized = {
[tv-dator]         +        operationId: operation.operationId,
[tv-dator]         +        statementType: operation.statementType || QUERY_OPERATION.UPDATE,
[tv-dator]         +        partitionIds: Array.isArray(operation.partitionIds) ?
[tv-dator]         +          [...operation.partitionIds] :
[tv-dator]         +          [],
[tv-dator]         +        idempotencyKey: operation.idempotencyKey || operation.operationId,
[tv-dator]         +        payloadHash:
[tv-dator]         +          operation.payloadHash || this.createWritePayloadHash(operation),
[tv-dator]         +        status: WRITE_OPERATION_STATUS.PENDING,
[tv-dator]         +        retryCount: 0,
[tv-dator]         +        lastError: null,
[tv-dator]         +        createdAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +        updatedAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]         +      tx.writeOperations.push(normalized);
[tv-dator]         +      tx.updatedAt = now;
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      await this.persistTransactionRecord(tx);
[tv-dator]         +      await this.persistWriteOperationRecord(tx, normalized);
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Mark one recorded write operation with execution outcome.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} operationId - Operation identifier.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} result - Aggregated write result.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<void>}
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async markWriteOperationResult(sessionId, operationId, result) {
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = this.transactionsBySession.get(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (!tx) {
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      const operation = tx.writeOperations.find(
[tv-dator]         +        (entry) => entry.operationId === operationId,
[tv-dator]         +      );
[tv-dator]         +      if (!operation) {
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      operation.status =
[tv-dator]         +        result?.success === true ?
[tv-dator]         +          WRITE_OPERATION_STATUS.SUCCEEDED :
[tv-dator]         +          WRITE_OPERATION_STATUS.FAILED;

[tv-dator]         +          PARTICIPANT_RETRY_DEFAULT.MAX_RETRIES;
[tv-dator]         +      this.participantRetryBaseDelayMs =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.participantRetryBaseDelayMs) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.participantRetryBaseDelayMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.participantRetryBaseDelayMs) :
[tv-dator]         +          PARTICIPANT_RETRY_DEFAULT.BASE_DELAY_MS;
[tv-dator]         +      this.participantRetryMaxDelayMs =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.participantRetryMaxDelayMs) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.participantRetryMaxDelayMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.participantRetryMaxDelayMs) :
[tv-dator]         +          PARTICIPANT_RETRY_DEFAULT.MAX_DELAY_MS;
[tv-dator]         +      this.sleep =
[tv-dator]         +        options.sleep ||
[tv-dator]         +        ((delayMs) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs)));
[tv-dator]         +      this.onParticipantRetry = options.onParticipantRetry || null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.logger = options.logger || console;
[tv-dator]         +      this.loadRecoveryStateForSweep = options.loadRecoveryStateForSweep || null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback =
[tv-dator]         +        typeof options.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback === 'function' ?
[tv-dator]         +          options.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback :
[tv-dator]         +          null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepIntervalMs =
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(options.recoverySweepIntervalMs) &&
[tv-dator]         +        options.recoverySweepIntervalMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]         +          Math.floor(options.recoverySweepIntervalMs) :
[tv-dator]         +          RECOVERY_SWEEP_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepTimer = null;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepInFlight = false;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs = 0;
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepDeferredAttempts = 0;
[tv-dator]         +      this.workflowCoordinator =
[tv-dator]         +        options.workflowCoordinator ||
[tv-dator]         +        new DurableWorkflowCoordinator({
[tv-dator]         +          // The transaction row is inserted once, before any participant
[tv-dator]         +          // work; every later write of it updates the existing row.
[tv-dator]         +          persistWorkflow: async (workflow) => {
[tv-dator]         +            await this.persistTransaction({
[tv-dator]         +              transactionId: workflow.transactionId || workflow.workflowId,
[tv-dator]         +              sessionId: workflow.sessionId || workflow.ownerKey,
[tv-dator]         +              status: workflow.status,
[tv-dator]         +              transactionEpoch: workflow.transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +              timeoutDeadline: workflow.timeoutDeadline,
[tv-dator]         +              transactionMode: workflow.transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +              participantSetState: workflow.participantSetState,
[tv-dator]         +              participantSetText: workflow.participantSetText,
[tv-dator]         +              commitMode: workflow.commitMode,
[tv-dator]         +              frozenParticipantCount: workflow.frozenParticipantCount,
[tv-dator]         +              createdAt: workflow.createdAt,
[tv-dator]         +              updatedAt: workflow.updatedAt,
[tv-dator]         +            }, {insertOnce: workflow.transactionRowInserted !== true});
[tv-dator]         +            workflow.transactionRowInserted = true;
[tv-dator]         +          },
[tv-dator]         +          persistParticipant: async (participant) => {
[tv-dator]         +            await this.persistParticipant({
[tv-dator]         +              participantId: participant.participantId,
[tv-dator]         +              transactionId: participant.transactionId || participant.workflowId,
[tv-dator]         +              partitionId: participant.partitionId || participant.participantKey,
[tv-dator]         +              status: participant.status,
[tv-dator]         +              lastError: participant.lastError,
[tv-dator]         +              preparedDigest: participant.preparedDigest ?? null,
[tv-dator]         +              prepareIndex: participant.prepareIndex ?? null,
[tv-dator]         +              prepareTerm: participant.prepareTerm ?? null,
[tv-dator]         +              createdAt: participant.createdAt,
[tv-dator]         +              updatedAt: participant.updatedAt,
[tv-dator]         +            });
[tv-dator]         +            if (participant.status === PARTICIPANT_STATUS.PREPARED) {
[tv-dator]         +              participant.prepareEvidenceRetained = true;
[tv-dator]         +            }
[tv-dator]         +          },
[tv-dator]         +          now: this.now,
[tv-dator]         +        });
[tv-dator]         +      this.transactionsBySession = this.workflowCoordinator.workflowsByOwnerKey;
[tv-dator]         +      // Each session's last ended transaction: a COMMIT that finds none reads
[tv-dator]         +      // whether that transaction reached its commit point from here.
[tv-dator]         +      this.endedTransactions = createEndedTransactionRecords();
[tv-dator]         +      this.transactionOperationTailBySession = new Map();
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoveredTransactionIds = new Set();
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Begin a distributed transaction for a session.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} [options] - {transactionMode, timeoutDeadline}: a
[tv-dator]         +     *   retried statement-autocommit attempt names its first attempt's
[tv-dator]         +     *   deadline and keeps it (TX1 seam V: one end-to-end budget).
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<Object>} Transaction begin result.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async begin(sessionId, options = {}) {
[tv-dator]         +      const expectedTransactionId = options.expectedTransactionId ??
[tv-dator]         +        this.getTransaction(sessionId)?.transactionId;
[tv-dator]         +      return this.runTransactionOperation(sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        () => this.beginOwned(sessionId, {...options, expectedTransactionId}));
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    async beginOwned(sessionId, options) {
[tv-dator]         +      const admission = admitExpectedTransaction(this, sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        options.expectedTransactionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (admission.state === STATEMENT_ADMISSION.REFUSED) return admission.failure;
[tv-dator]         +      if (this.transactionsBySession.has(sessionId)) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.TRANSACTION_ACTIVE,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.TRANSACTION_ACTIVE,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      let transactionEpoch;
[tv-dator]         +      try {
[tv-dator]         +        transactionEpoch = this.epochSource();
[tv-dator]         +      } catch (_err) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.BEGIN_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.INTERNAL_ERROR,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +      if (!isCanonicalTransactionEpoch(transactionEpoch)) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.BEGIN_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.INTERNAL_ERROR,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      const timeoutBudget = createTopLevelOperationBudget({
[tv-dator]         +        configuredBudgetMs: this.transactionBudgetMs,
[tv-dator]         +        operationName: QUERY_OPERATION.TRANSACTION,
[tv-dator]         +        now: this.now,
[tv-dator]         +        startedAtMs: Number.isFinite(options.timeoutDeadline) ?
[tv-dator]         +          options.timeoutDeadline - this.transactionBudgetMs : this.now(),
[tv-dator]         +      });
[tv-dator]         +      const transactionMode =
[tv-dator]         +        options.transactionMode === TRANSACTION_MODE.AUTOCOMMIT ?
[tv-dator]         +          TRANSACTION_MODE.AUTOCOMMIT :
[tv-dator]         +          TRANSACTION_MODE.EXPLICIT;
[tv-dator]         +      return this.registerUniqueTransaction(sessionId, {
[tv-dator]         +        transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget, transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +      });
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    async registerUniqueTransaction(sessionId, fields) {
[tv-dator]         +      const {transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget, transactionMode} = fields;
[tv-dator]         +      for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt += 1) {
[tv-dator]         +        const transactionId = this.createTransactionId(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +        try {
[tv-dator]         +          await this.registerTransaction(sessionId, transactionId, {
[tv-dator]         +            transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +            timeoutBudget,
[tv-dator]         +            transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +          });
[tv-dator]         +        } catch (error) {
[tv-dator]         +          if (error?.code === QUERY_ERROR_CODE.TRANSACTION_RECOVERY_INCOMPLETE &&
[tv-dator]         +              error.advancedTransactionRow &&
[tv-dator]         +              this.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback !== null) {
[tv-dator]         +            try {
[tv-dator]         +              this.retainAdvancedTransactionReadback(
[tv-dator]         +                error.advancedTransactionRow,
[tv-dator]         +              );
[tv-dator]         +            } catch (recoveryError) {
[tv-dator]         +              this.logger?.warn?.(QUERY_ERROR_MSG.TRANSACTION_RECOVERY_INCOMPLETE,
[tv-dator]         +                {transactionId, error: recoveryError?.message});
[tv-dator]         +            }
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]         +          // Only a confirmed collision of the minted identity re-mints; any
[tv-dator]         +          // other persistence failure is a typed BEGIN failure (seam B/S).
[tv-dator]         +          if (isTransactionIdCollision(error) &&
[tv-dator]         +              attempt < TRANSACTION_ID_MINT_ATTEMPTS) {
[tv-dator]         +            continue;
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]         +          return {
[tv-dator]         +            success: false,
[tv-dator]         +            error: error?.message || QUERY_ERROR_MSG.BEGIN_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +            errorCode: error?.code || error?.errorCode ||
[tv-dator]         +              QUERY_ERROR_CODE.TRANSACTION_STATE_PERSIST_FAILED,
[tv-dator]         +          };
[tv-dator]         +        }
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: true,
[tv-dator]         +          operation: QUERY_OPERATION.BEGIN_TRANSACTION,
[tv-dator]         +          sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +          transactionId,
[tv-dator]         +          transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +          transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Insert the transaction's row once, before any participant work, and
[tv-dator]         +     * hold the transaction for its session.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} transactionId - The minted identity.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} fields - {transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget,
[tv-dator]         +     *   transactionMode}.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<void>}
[tv-dator]         +     * @private
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async registerTransaction(sessionId, transactionId, fields) {
[tv-dator]         +      const {transactionEpoch, timeoutBudget, transactionMode} = fields;
[tv-dator]         +      const now = this.now();
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = {
[tv-dator]         +        sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        ownerKey: sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionId,
[tv-dator]         +        workflowId: transactionId,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +        timeoutBudget,
[tv-dator]         +        timeoutDeadline: timeoutBudget.deadlineMs,
[tv-dator]         +        status: TRANSACTION_STATUS.ACTIVE,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetState: PARTICIPANT_SET_STATE.OPEN,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetText: JSON.stringify([]),
[tv-dator]         +        commitMode: COMMIT_MODE.NOT_SELECTED,
[tv-dator]         +        frozenParticipantCount: 0,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetFrozenAt: 0,
[tv-dator]         +        decisionTrace: [],
[tv-dator]         +        participants: new Map(),
[tv-dator]         +        writeOperations: [],
[tv-dator]         +        createdAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +        updatedAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]         +      await this.workflowCoordinator.registerWorkflow(tx);
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Enlist partition participants and begin transaction on new participants.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string[]} partitionIds - Target partition IDs.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<Object>} Enlistment result.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async enlistParticipants(sessionId, partitionIds,
[tv-dator]         +      expectedTransactionId = this.getTransaction(sessionId)?.transactionId) {
[tv-dator]         +      return this.runTransactionOperation(
[tv-dator]         +        sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        () => {
[tv-dator]         +          const admission = admitExpectedTransaction(this, sessionId, expectedTransactionId);
[tv-dator]         +          return admission.state === STATEMENT_ADMISSION.REFUSED ? admission.failure :
[tv-dator]         +            this.enlistParticipantsOwned(sessionId, partitionIds);
[tv-dator]         +        },
[tv-dator]         +      );
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Record distributed write operation metadata under a transaction.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} operation - Operation metadata.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<void>}
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async recordWriteOperation(sessionId, operation) {
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = this.transactionsBySession.get(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (!tx) {
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      const now = this.now();
[tv-dator]         +      const normalized = {
[tv-dator]         +        operationId: operation.operationId,
[tv-dator]         +        statementType: operation.statementType || QUERY_OPERATION.UPDATE,
[tv-dator]         +        partitionIds: Array.isArray(operation.partitionIds) ?
[tv-dator]         +          [...operation.partitionIds] :
[tv-dator]         +          [],
[tv-dator]         +        idempotencyKey: operation.idempotencyKey || operation.operationId,
[tv-dator]         +        payloadHash:
[tv-dator]         +          operation.payloadHash || this.createWritePayloadHash(operation),
[tv-dator]         +        status: WRITE_OPERATION_STATUS.PENDING,
[tv-dator]         +        retryCount: 0,
[tv-dator]         +        lastError: null,
[tv-dator]         +        createdAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +        updatedAt: now,
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]         +      tx.writeOperations.push(normalized);
[tv-dator]         +      tx.updatedAt = now;
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      await this.persistTransactionRecord(tx);
[tv-dator]         +      await this.persistWriteOperationRecord(tx, normalized);
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Mark one recorded write operation with execution outcome.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} operationId - Operation identifier.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} result - Aggregated write result.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<void>}
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async markWriteOperationResult(sessionId, operationId, result) {
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = this.transactionsBySession.get(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (!tx) {
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      const operation = tx.writeOperations.find(
[tv-dator]         +        (entry) => entry.operationId === operationId,
[tv-dator]         +      );
[tv-dator]         +      if (!operation) {
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      operation.status =
[tv-dator]         +        result?.success === true ?
[tv-dator]         +          WRITE_OPERATION_STATUS.SUCCEEDED :
[tv-dator]         +          WRITE_OPERATION_STATUS.FAILED;
[tv-dator]         +      operation.retryCount = Number.isInteger(result?.retryCount) ?
[tv-dator]         +        result.retryCount :
[tv-dator]         +        0;
[tv-dator]         +      operation.lastError =
[tv-dator]         +        result?.success === true ?
[tv-dator]         +          null :
[tv-dator]         +          result?.error || QUERY_ERROR_MSG.DISTRIBUTED_PARTICIPANT_FAILURE;
[tv-dator]         +      operation.updatedAt = this.now();
[tv-dator]         +      tx.updatedAt = operation.updatedAt;
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +      await this.persistTransactionRecord(tx);
[tv-dator]         +      await this.persistWriteOperationRecord(tx, operation);
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Commit a distributed transaction across all enlisted participants.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} [options] - {expectedTransactionId}: the COMMIT's own
[tv-dator]         +     *   transaction, when the caller knows it.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<Object>} Commit result.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async commit(sessionId, options = {}) {
[tv-dator]         +      const expectedTransactionId = options.expectedTransactionId ??
[tv-dator]         +        this.getTransaction(sessionId)?.transactionId ?? null;
[tv-dator]         +      return this.runTransactionOperation(
[tv-dator]         +        sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        () => this.commitOwned(sessionId, expectedTransactionId),
[tv-dator]         +      );
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    async commitOwned(sessionId, expectedTransactionId = null) {
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = this.transactionsBySession.get(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (!tx || tx.transactionId !== expectedTransactionId) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.NO_TRANSACTION_COMMIT,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.NO_TRANSACTION,
[tv-dator]         +          ...this.endedTransactions.commitPointFor(sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +            expectedTransactionId),
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +      await this.freezeParticipantSet(tx);
[tv-dator]         +      try {
[tv-dator]         +        return withCommitPoint(await this.runCommitProtocol(tx), tx);
[tv-dator]         +      } catch (error) {
[tv-dator]         +        throw stampThrownCommitPoint(error, tx);
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Roll back a distributed transaction across all enlisted participants.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<Object>} Rollback result.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    async rollback(sessionId, options = {}) {
[tv-dator]         +      const expectedTransactionId = options.expectedTransactionId ??
[tv-dator]         +        this.getTransaction(sessionId)?.transactionId ?? null;
[tv-dator]         +      return this.runTransactionOperation(
[tv-dator]         +        sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        () => this.rollbackOwned(sessionId, expectedTransactionId),
[tv-dator]         +      );
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    async rollbackOwned(sessionId, expectedTransactionId) {
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = this.transactionsBySession.get(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (!tx || tx.transactionId !== expectedTransactionId) {
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]         +          success: false,
[tv-dator]         +          error: QUERY_ERROR_MSG.NO_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK,
[tv-dator]         +          errorCode: QUERY_ERROR_CODE.NO_TRANSACTION,
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +      return this.runRollbackProtocol(tx);
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Return transaction metadata for one session.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Object|null} Transaction metadata.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    getTransaction(sessionId) {
[tv-dator]         +      const tx = this.transactionsBySession.get(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +      if (!tx) {
[tv-dator]         +        return null;
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]         +      return {
[tv-dator]         +        sessionId: tx.sessionId,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionId: tx.transactionId,
[tv-dator]         +        status: tx.status,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionEpoch: tx.transactionEpoch,
[tv-dator]         +        timeoutDeadline: tx.timeoutDeadline,
[tv-dator]         +        transactionMode: tx.transactionMode,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetState: tx.participantSetState,
[tv-dator]         +        commitMode: tx.commitMode,
[tv-dator]         +        frozenParticipantCount: tx.frozenParticipantCount,
[tv-dator]         +        participantSetFrozenAt: tx.participantSetFrozenAt,
[tv-dator]         +        decisionTrace: tx.decisionTrace.map((entry) => ({...entry})),
[tv-dator]         +        participants: this.getOrderedParticipantIds(tx),
[tv-dator]         +        participantDetails: this.getOrderedParticipantDetails(tx),
[tv-dator]         +        writeOperations: tx.writeOperations.map((operation) => ({...operation})),
[tv-dator]         +        createdAt: tx.createdAt,
[tv-dator]         +        updatedAt: tx.updatedAt,
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]         +     * Check if a session has an active transaction.
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {string} sessionId - Session ID.
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {boolean} Active transaction state.
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]         +    hasActiveTransaction(sessionId) {
[tv-dator]         +      return this.transactionsBySession.has(sessionId);
[tv-dator]         +    }
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]         +    async setTransactionStatus(tx, status) {
[tv-dator]         +      return setTransactionStatus.call(this, tx, status);
[tv-dator]         +    }

[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          decisionReadState: protocolResult?.decisionReadState ?? null,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          failedParticipants: protocolResult?.failedParticipants || [],
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        });
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        if (protocolResult?.success === true || skipped) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          this.recoveredTransactionIds.delete(workflowId);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const resumed = results.filter(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        (entry) => entry.success && !entry.skipped,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      ).length;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const failed = results.filter((entry) => !entry.success).length;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      return {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        totalRecovered: recoveredWorkflowIds.length,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        resumed,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        failed,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        results,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * Run one recovery sweep cycle for timed-out non-terminal transactions.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Promise<Object>} Sweep summary.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    async runRecoverySweep() {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      if (this.recoverySweepInFlight) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          swept: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          resolved: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          failed: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          skipped: true,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          deferred: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          results: [],
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      if (this.isRecoverySweepDeferred()) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          swept: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          resolved: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          failed: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          skipped: false,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          deferred: 1,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          deferredUntilMs: this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          results: [],
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepInFlight = true;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      try {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        if (typeof this.loadRecoveryStateForSweep === LOCAL_STR_FUNCTION) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          let payload;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          try {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            payload = await this.loadRecoveryStateForSweep();
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          } catch (error) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            // Discovery is a hint source. A failed cache census cannot
[tv-dator]         suppress
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            // the authoritative retries of IDs already retained by this
[tv-dator]         owner.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            this.logger?.warn?.(RECOVERY_SWEEP_LOG_MSG, {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              error: error?.message || String(error),
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              retainedTransactions: this.recoveredTransactionIds.size,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            });
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          if (payload && typeof payload === LOCAL_STR_OBJECT) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            this.recoverFromSystemTables(payload);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        const stuckTransactions = Array.from(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          this.workflowCoordinator.workflowsById.values(),
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        ).filter(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          (tx) =>
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            tx.recoveryDiscovered ||
[tv-dator]         (!TERMINAL_TRANSACTION_STATUS.has(tx.status) &&
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              this.isTransactionBudgetExceeded(tx)),
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        );
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        const results = [];
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        let deferred = 0;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        for (const tx of stuckTransactions) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          let protocolResult = null;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          let sweepPath = null;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          try {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            await this.runTransactionOperation(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              tx.sessionId,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              async () => {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                if (!tx.recoveryDiscovered &&
[tv-dator]         TERMINAL_TRANSACTION_STATUS.has(tx.status)) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                  protocolResult = {success: true};
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                  return;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                const redriven = await redriveRecoveredTransaction(this, tx);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                sweepPath = redriven.path;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                protocolResult = redriven.result;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            );
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          } catch (error) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            if (this.shouldDeferRecoverySweepError(error)) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              deferred += 1;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              const deferredResult = this.buildDeferredRecoverySweepResult(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                error,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                  swept: stuckTransactions.length,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                  results: [
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                    {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                      transactionId: tx.transactionId,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                      sessionId: tx.sessionId,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                      sweepPath,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                      statusAfter: tx.status,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                      success: false,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                      deferred: true,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                      error: error?.message || String(error),
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                  ],
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              );
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              results.push(...deferredResult.results);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              continue;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            throw error;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          if (
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            protocolResult?.success !== true &&
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            this.shouldDeferRecoverySweepError(protocolResult)
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          ) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            deferred += 1;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            this.deferRecoverySweep(protocolResult);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            results.push({
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              transactionId: tx.transactionId,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              sessionId: tx.sessionId,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              sweepPath,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              statusAfter: tx.status,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              success: false,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              deferred: true,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              error: protocolResult?.error || null,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            });
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            continue;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          results.push({
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            transactionId: tx.transactionId,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            sessionId: tx.sessionId,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            sweepPath,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            statusAfter: tx.status,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            success: protocolResult?.success === true,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            error: protocolResult?.error || null,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          });
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        const resolved = results.filter((entry) => entry.success).length;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        const failed = results.filter(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          (entry) => entry.success !== true && entry.deferred !== true,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        ).length;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        if (deferred === 0) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          this.clearRecoverySweepDeferState();
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        return {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          swept: stuckTransactions.length,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          resolved,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          failed,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          deferred,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          deferredUntilMs: deferred > 0 ?
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs :
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          skipped: false,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          results,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        };
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      } finally {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        this.recoverySweepInFlight = false;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * Start periodic transaction recovery sweep.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    startRecoverySweep() {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      if (this.recoverySweepTimer) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      }

[tv-dator]         +        void this.runRecoverySweep().catch((error) => {
[tv-dator]

[tv-dator]

[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          });
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        });
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      }, this.recoverySweepIntervalMs);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepTimer.unref();
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * Stop periodic transaction recovery sweep.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    stopRecoverySweep() {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      if (!this.recoverySweepTimer) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        return;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      clearInterval(this.recoverySweepTimer);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepTimer = null;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {boolean}
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @private
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    isRecoverySweepDeferred() {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      return (
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        Number.isFinite(this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs) &&
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs > this.now()
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      );
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {*} errorLike
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {{delayMs: number, deferredUntilMs: number}}
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @private
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    deferRecoverySweep(errorLike) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const retryAfterMs = getControlPlaneRetryAfterMs(errorLike);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const backoffMs =
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        retryAfterMs > 0 ?
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          retryAfterMs :
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          Math.min(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            RECOVERY_SWEEP_DEFER_MAX_MS,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            Math.max(
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              this.recoverySweepIntervalMs,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +              RECOVERY_SWEEP_DEFER_BASE_MS *
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +                  2 ** this.recoverySweepDeferredAttempts,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +            ),
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          );
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepDeferredAttempts += 1;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs = this.now() + backoffMs;
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      return {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        delayMs: backoffMs,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        deferredUntilMs: this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {void}
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @private
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    clearRecoverySweepDeferState() {
[tv-dator]

[tv-dator]

[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {*} errorLike
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} [overrides={}]
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @return {Object}
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @private
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    buildDeferredRecoverySweepResult(errorLike, overrides = {}) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      this.deferRecoverySweep(errorLike);
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      return {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        swept: overrides.swept || 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        resolved: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        failed: 0,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        skipped: false,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        deferred: Number.isFinite(overrides.deferred) ?
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          overrides.deferred :
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          1,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        deferredUntilMs: this.recoverySweepDeferredUntilMs,
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        error: errorLike?.message || String(errorLike),
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        results: Array.isArray(overrides.results) ? overrides.results : [],
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      };
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    },
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    /**
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * Recover coordinator state from canonical system-table rows.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     *
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object} payload - Recovery payload.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object[]} [payload.transactions] - sql_transactions rows.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object[]} [payload.participants] -
[tv-dator]         sql_transaction_participants
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     *   rows.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object[]} [payload.writeOperations] - sql_write_operations
[tv-dator]         rows.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     * @param {Object[]} [payload.decisions] - recorded decision rows
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     *   ({transaction_id, decision, decision_text, decision_digest}); a
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     *   sql_transactions row carries its own in the same columns.
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +     */
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +  
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +    recoverFromSystemTables(payload = {}) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const transactionRows = Array.isArray(payload.transactions) ?
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        payload.transactions :
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        [];
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const participantRows = Array.isArray(payload.participants) ?
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        payload.participants :
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        [];
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const writeOperationRows = Array.isArray(payload.writeOperations) ?
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        payload.writeOperations :
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        [];
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      const knownIds = new
[tv-dator]         Set(this.workflowCoordinator.workflowsById.keys());
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +      for (const row of transactionRows) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        if (!(row.transaction_id || row.transactionId) || !(row.session_id ||
[tv-dator]         row.sessionId)) {
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          throw buildTransactionRecoveryIncompleteError({decisionDimension:
[tv-dator]         null, row});
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        }
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        const transactionEpoch = row[LOCAL_STR_TRANSACTION_EPOCH] ??
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +          row[LOCAL_STR_TRANSACTIONEPOCH];
[tv-dator]       
[tv-dator]         +        if (!isCanonicalTransactionEpoch(transactionEpoch)) {
